⌂ Introduction
==============

Authentication made for everyone![#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/#authentication-made-for-everyone)

================================================================================================

Secure, scalable, and game-changing authentication for your applications. Get started in minutes with our powerful APIs and SDKs.

ⓘ What is KeyAuth?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/#%E2%93%98-what-is-keyauth)

==========================================================================

KeyAuth is an authentication platform for applications that require a user to log in.

Applications are the backbone of the service. Applications are responsible for storing the information of each user who is registered to use your service.

It is important to note that KeyAuth is solely an authentication service and **not** and obfuscation service. Clients are still resonsbile for the security of their overall application.

What can KeyAuth be used for?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/#what-can-keyauth-be-used-for)

========================================================================================

Regardless of the service, KeyAuth can be used for just about anything, including, but not limited to:

Reseller Systems

Game Development

Website Development

PC applications

Mobile applications

and more. If the language you work with can make a request, KeyAuth can be used in it!

Which would you like to focus on?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/#which-would-you-like-to-focus-on)

================================================================================================

[](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api)

Client API

The client API is the primary API used in every application. This is used to allow a user to register an account and log into your website or application.

[](https://docs.keyauth.cc/seller-api)

Seller API

The Seller API is a secondary API given to our users that are on our Seller Plan.

The Seller API allows you to control your application from anywhere! Create your own dashboard, manage all of your users and much more!

You must have the seller subscription in order to use the seller api. - Seller API \[Application Settings\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/folder-819666.md): - Custom Domain Setup \[Custom Domain (API) \](https://docs.keyauth.cc/customdomain/api.md): - Custom Domain Setup \[Custom Domain (Customer Panel)\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/customdomain/customerpanel.md): ## API Docs - API > Getting Started > License \[KeyAuth - License Login\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/license/license-login.md): Register and log in with just a license. Once the license is used for the first time, users will only need to use the license to access the application. - API > Getting Started > License \[KeyAuth - Redeem license (upgrade)\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/license/redeem-license.md): Assign an unused license to a user. - API > Getting Started \[KeyAuth - Initialization\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/initialization.md): :::info - API > Getting Started \[KeyAuth - Login with user/pass\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/login.md): Log into the application using a username and password. - API > Getting Started \[KeyAuth - Logout\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/logout.md): Log out of the application. This will end the session and require the user to login again. - API > Getting Started \[KeyAuth - Register\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/register.md): Register an account using a username, password, license, and email (optional) - API > Getting Started \[Upgrade\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/upgrade.md): Gives the users a subscription that has the same level as the key. - API > Getting Started \[KeyAuth - Login with email/pass\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/loginemail.md): Log into the application using a email and password. - API > Getting Started \[Email OTP - Send Code\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/emailotp-send.md): Log into the application using email OTP (one time password). You will receive an email with a 6 digit verification code. - API > Getting Started \[Email OTP - Verify Code\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/emailotp-verify.md): Log into the application using email OTP (one time password). You will receive an email with a 6 digit verification code then you will use this function to verify the 6 digit code. - API > Getting Started \[Magic Link - Send Code\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/magiclink-send.md): Send a passwordless login link to a user's email address. The application must first initialize a KeyAuth session. After this request succeeds, the user will receive an email containing a single-use login button. The link will expire after 15 minutes. After sending it, use the \`magicLinkCheck\` operation to wait for the user to approve the login. - API > Getting Started \[Magic Link - Check Status\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/magiclink-check.md): This is the dection for Magic Link (Send). It will verify the status as well as the token and proceed with the request if everything is verified properly. - API > Features > Chatroom/Messages \[KeyAuth - Retrieve Messages\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/chatroom/messages/retrieve-messages.md): Receive messages from the chat channel. - API > Features > Chatroom/Messages \[KeyAuth - Send Messages\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/chatroom/messages/send-messages.md): Allow users to send messages to a chat channel. - API > Features > User Variables \[KeyAuth - Retrieve User Variable\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/user/variables/retrieve-user-variable.md): Retrieve the user variable data assigned to the user - API > Features > User Variables \[KeyAuth - Set User Variable\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/user/variables/set-user-variable.md): Set a user variable. This variable contains data that will be accessible by this user. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Ban User\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/ban-user.md): Ban a user from your application - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Check Blacklist\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/check-blacklist.md): Check if the user is blacklisted or not. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Check Session\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/check-session.md): Check to see if a session is valid or not - API > Features \[Disable 2FA\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/disable2fa.md): Disable Two Factor Authentication(2fa) on a client account. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Change Username\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/change-username.md): Allow the user to change their username. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Download File\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/download-file.md): Allow users to downloaded files. - API > Features \[Enable 2FA\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/enable2fa.md): Enable Two Factor Authentication(2fa) on a client account. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Fetch Online Users\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/fetch-online-users.md): Fetch all online users. (active sessions) - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Forgot Password\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/forgot-password.md): Allow users to reset their password if they forgot it. The user must have entered an email when registering an account or else they will be unable to reset their password until an email is assigned. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Fetch Stats\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/fetch-stats.md): Run this function first to retrieve all stored stats. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Log\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/log.md): Log events from your application. - API > Features \[KeyAuth - Webhook\](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/features/webhook.md): Send requests securely from KeyAuth server - ⛨ Security Practices
====================

Security Tips[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/security-practices#security-tips)

====================================================================================

INFO

⚠️ KeyAuth protects your licensing and authentication. But protecting your program is something you also need to take steps on. We are an authentication service, not an obfuscation service ⚠️

When you use the official KeyAuth API libraries, the following protections are already built in:

**Signed Responses** : Every response from the server is signed with Ed25519. The client checks this signature - attackers cannot fake a "success" reply without the real server's private key.

**Timestamp Verification** : Replies include a timestamp to prevent replaying old requests. The client checks that the timestamp is valid before accepting the response.

**Session Handling** : Sessions expire automatically. Even if someone grabs a request, it won't work later.

In other words: it's not just a simple "if" check. Your program only proceeds if the response is signed and timestamped.

\--

### What you should do as a Developer[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/security-practices#what-you-should-do-as-a-developer)

1.

**Do not remove checks** : The checks that we have in place (timestamp, signature, etc.) should remain in your program

2.

If altering the API file use HTTPS safely. Add certificate pinning to stop man-in-the-middle atacks.

3.

Spread out security checks. Don't rely on a check just on startup. A security check should ideally be ran every 30sec-1min and in multiple places in your code.

4.

Do not hard code senstive data.

5.

Obfuscate your code. Even basic obfuscation/packing makes it harder for an attacker to gain access to your program. Adding anti-debug/anti-inject is highly recommended if possible.

6.

Update your program often. The longer your program stays "as is", the more time an attacker has to learn more about it and plan an attack.

**These don't make your program "uncrackable", but they slow attackers down. Nothing is "uncrackable".**

There is **no** point in doing the steps below if you're sending requests from a server. As servers will not need this protection as the user will not be able to modify the network traffic between the server and the KeyAuth API.

GUID Setup[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/security-practices#guid-setup)

==============================================================================

You are going to want to include a random **GUID** in the `enckey` paramater with the initialization request to the **1.2 endpoint**. Make sure that the length of the GUID is 36 characters or less. You will send this GUID in the enckey paramater only on init (initialization), none of the other requests.

Then you will want to store this GUID along with your application secret for later reference. Example: `[GUID]-[secret]`.

So, if my GUID that I passed in the enckey parameter was `4a59f8ca-b304-47` and my application secret is `76489f2ba92ddf9132e28d56870004a62d30ec5b40eaf2071ae48036e7144b5f`, I would want to store the string `4a59f8ca-b304-47-76489f2ba92ddf9132e28d56870004a62d30ec5b40eaf2071ae48036e7144b5f` for later use for the initialization response.

For initialization request responses, there is a signature header which is a `SHA256 HMAC` of the JSON response encoded with the application secret.

So the header is signature: `8d0a11b00f44bee4e563117db28533943f5170854f5f65e69470c59bffb7d0d5` and the JSON response is:

    {
        "success":true,
        "message":"Initialized",
        "sessionid":"b8Q1f62SdW",
        "appinfo":
        {
            "numUsers":"6",
            "numOnlineUsers":"120",
            "numKeys":"1533",
            "version":"1.0",
            "customerPanelLink":"https:\/\/localhost\/panel\/wnelson03\/test\/"
        }
     }

Encoding the above JSON with my application secret **76489f2ba92ddf9132e28d56870004a62d30ec5b40eaf2071ae48036e7144b5f** on the website [HMAC SHA256 ONLINE](https://www.javainuse.com/hmac)
 I get the following output: `8d0a11b00f44bee4e563117db28533943f5170854f5f65e69470c59bffb7d0d5`. In my application, I compare these and abort the program if the **do not** match.

For all other responses, you're going to need the string that you saved earlier, `[GUID]-[secret]`.

My string is `4a59f8ca-b304-47-76489f2ba92ddf9132e28d56870004a62d30ec5b40eaf2071ae48036e7144b5f` and the JSON response is:

    {
        "success":true,
        "message":"Logged in!",
        "info":
        {
            "username":"3Y8FC2-MRHKUO-U9RH8I-GNHD2U-THK4X8-PW584D",
            "subscriptions":
            [\
                {\
                    "subscription":"default",\
                    "key":"3Y8FC2-MRHKUO-U9RH8I-GNHD2U-THK4X8-PW584D",\
                    "expiry":"1659657607"\
                 },\
                 {\
                     "subscription":"default",\
                     "key":null,\
                     "expiry":"1659749662"\
                 }\
             ],
             "ip":"::1",
             "hwid":null,
             "createdate":"1659225608",
             "lastlogin":"1659363059"
        }
    }

and the header that I received was signature: `e2993347290077c90011694bf887975117bff08897106f9c501d75c48102f721`

Encoding the JSON response that I received with the string that I saved before I made the init request, `4a59f8ca-b304-47-76489f2ba92ddf9132e28d56870004a62d30ec5b40eaf2071ae48036e7144b5f` on the website [HMAC SHA256 ONLINE](https://www.javainuse.com/hmac)
, I get the same result as the signature `e2993347290077c90011694bf887975117bff08897106f9c501d75c48102f721`

==========================

Premade Examples[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/examples#premade-examples)

================================================================================

No need to start from scratch. With our premade examples you can easily insert your code, application credentials and your all set!

INFO

Only our C++, C# and Python examples are updated to our newest 1.3 endpoint. All other examples use 1.0-1.2.

? QnA
=====

General

OAuth Login

What is the difference between keyauth.win and keyauth.cc?

While KeyAuth.cc and KeyAuth.win are both owned by KeyAuth LLC. One focuses more on the API (keyauth.win). The API cannot be accessed through KeyAuth.cc. Other than that, the sites function the same exact way.

Why does KeyAuth.cc load for me, but KeyAuth.win doesn't?

If KeyAuth.cc loads, but KeyAuth.win doesn't it could be due to a tampered HOST file, or a strict ISP depending on the region you are in.

Reports show users in Russia and Brazil have the most issues when trying to connect.

You may also check your HOST file (located in System32) to ensure it hasn't been tampered with. If you see "keyauth" in the file, you can more than likely just remove it and then try to access the website again. It should work fine.

I'm calling the \`init\` function, but my program still says that it's not being called. Why?

If you're calling the `init` function inside your program, but an error is still popping up saying otherwise, you are more than likely calling it in the wrong location.

The application credentials and the `init` function must be called on the same script. While also ensuring that the `init` function is only being called one(1) time!

Why does KeyAuth have so many domains?

[https://keyauth.win](https://keyauth.win/)

[https://keyauth.cc](https://keyauth.cc/)

[https://keyauth.com](https://keyauth.com/)
 (redirect)

[https://keyauth.uk](https://keyauth.uk/)
 (redirect)

We have a fair amount of domains, and while two of them are redirects it is because certain users based on their region will have better chances of being able to access the website on one rather than the other. The primary ones are **.win** and **.cc**.

If you visit any other domain that looks just like KeyAuth and are not on this list, they are more than likely attempting to steal your credentials. Please report the website to our support team via our [Ticket System](https://keyauth.cc/)
.

Manage Applications
===================

💡

### Application Credentials[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#application-credentials)

Application credentials are needed for your program to connect to our service properly. Without valid application credentials, you would not be able to send proper data for your users or your program. The most common credentials used are:

**Application Name** - the name of your application

**Owner ID** - your account owner identication code

**Application Secret** - this is only used in endpoints other than 1.3

**Application Version** - the version of your application

**Token Path** - the path for an application/user access token. This is not the same thing as the user license, which is required to access a program.

INFO

It's important to note that not every programming language will require the same credentials.

💡

### Creating An Application[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#creating-an-application)

An application is required to view the majority of all of the pages on the dashboard. An application will allow you to manage your users, chats, and extra data from your program.

💡

### Selecting an Application[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#selecting-an-application)

In order to access all of the pages we have to offer, once you create an application, you must then select the application. You can do so by simply clicking on "Select" in the Applications Table.

💡

### Renaming An Application[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#renaming-an-application)

Users can rename their application at any time. By renaming the application, all data is transferred over. Users must simply change the application name in their program in order for the change to completely successfully.

💡

### Pausing an Application/Users[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#pausing-an-applicationusers)

Temporarily disables your application and prevents users from accessing it. While paused, the app becomes unusable until you manually unpause it.

💡

### Unpausing an Application/Users[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#unpausing-an-applicationusers)

Reenables your application and allows access for all users, making it fully functional again.

💡

### Refreshing an Application Secret[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#refreshing-an-application-secret)

Generates a new application secret. Required only for API version 1.2 and below. You must update your program with the new secret to maintain functionality.

💡

### Deleting an Application[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/manage-applications#deleting-an-application)

Permanently deletes your application and all its data. This action cannot be undone. To confirm, you must type the application's name in the provided text box.

◷ Rate Limiting
===============

What is Rate Limiting?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/rate-limiting#what-is-rate-limiting)

================================================================================================

Rate limiting in short, is used to control the rate(number) of requests sent or received by a network.

Why is Rate Limiting a thing?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/rate-limiting#why-is-rate-limiting-a-thing)

==============================================================================================================

Since KeyAuth LLC provides an API service, it's important to ensure the best connectivity for all users and prevent a "bloated" server. In order to do this, we must limit the amount of requests a user can make within a specific time frame. This also comes in handy when beginner programmers first work with our API and aren't quite sure how to properly function it.

What is our Rate Limit?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/rate-limiting#what-is-our-rate-limit)

==================================================================================================

Users can make **30 requests within 30 seconds** before being rate limited. Any amount higher than 30 within 30 seconds will result in an error:

    {
        "status": false,
        "message": "You've been rate limited, you're sending too many requests"
    }
    

How do I get rid of my Rate Limit?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/overview/rate-limiting#how-do-i-get-rid-of-my-rate-limit)

========================================================================================================================

If you find yourself rate limited, do not worry! Simply wait 30minutes - 1hour and then try your request again. If you're still rate limited, you may need to contact our support team via [Discord](https://keyauth.cc/discord)
, [Telegram](https://t.me/keyauth)
 or our [Website](https://keyauth.cc/)
.

⌂ Introduction
==============

Authentication made for everyone![#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/getting-started/introduction#authentication-made-for-everyone)

============================================================================================================================

Secure, scalable, and game-changing authentication for your applications. Get started in minutes with our powerful APIs and SDKs.

ⓘ What is KeyAuth?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/getting-started/introduction#%E2%93%98-what-is-keyauth)

======================================================================================================

KeyAuth is an authentication platform for applications that require a user to log in.

Applications are the backbone of the service. Applications are responsible for storing the information of each user who is registered to use your service.

It is important to note that KeyAuth is solely an authentication service and **not** and obfuscation service. Clients are still resonsbile for the security of their overall application.

What can KeyAuth be used for?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/getting-started/introduction#what-can-keyauth-be-used-for)

====================================================================================================================

Regardless of the service, KeyAuth can be used for just about anything, including, but not limited to:

Reseller Systems

Game Development

Website Development

PC applications

Mobile applications

and more. If the language you work with can make a request, KeyAuth can be used in it!

Which would you like to focus on?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/getting-started/introduction#which-would-you-like-to-focus-on)

============================================================================================================================

[](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api)

Client API

The client API is the primary API used in every application. This is used to allow a user to register an account and log into your website or application.

[](https://docs.keyauth.cc/seller-api)

Seller API

The Seller API is a secondary API given to our users that are on our Seller Plan.

The Seller API allows you to control your application from anywhere! Create your own dashboard, manage all of your users and much more!

Users
=====

What are users?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/users#what-are-users)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Users are either created via the users page on the dashboard or via our API using the `.license` or `.register` function.

Creating a user[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/users#creating-a-user)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/372982/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

When creating a user, you are able to assign the following:

Username: The username for the user

Password: The password for the user, please note that if you would like the user to enter their own password for their account, then leave the password field empty. When the user goes to log into their account then can enter any password the first time.

Email: The email for the user. Emails are not required, however they are strongly recommended since they can help the user reset their password if they forget it down the road.

Subscription: The subscription that the user is having assigned to their account. Learn more about subscriptions on the subscriptions page.

Expiration: The date/time the users subscription is set to expire.

HWID affected: If you have HWID check enabled in your application settings but do not want a user to be affected by that check you can disable this feature, otherwise keep it enabled so they are forced to go through the check. This will help prevent users from sharing their account.

Resetting a users hwid[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/users#resetting-a-users-hwid)

==============================================================================================

If a user needs to reset their hwid for any reason, you can select their profile and then click on `reset hwid`. This will allow the next HWID they use to sign into the program to be assigned to their account.

Resetting all users hwids[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/users#resetting-all-users-hwids)

====================================================================================================

If all user hwids need to be reset, you can click on the yellow `reset all hwids` button. This will remove every HWID assigned to all users assigned to the application.

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373453/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Sessions
========

What are sessions?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/sessions#what-are-sessions)

========================================================================================

Sessions are used to ensure a user is validated properly when using the application. On [⚠️ Initialization ⚠️](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/initialization)
, a session ID is generated for the user. By default it is set to `Validated: False`. Once a user logs in successfully, it will change the status to `Validated: True`.

Unique sessions[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/sessions#unique-sessions)

===================================================================================

Sessions are unique. Users are only allow one(1) unique session per application run. If a user logs in and then closes the program and the `logout` function was not invoked then the next session they start will overwrite the initial session. This is done to prevent session spam/abuse.

💡

Application developers: It is strongly encouraged you use the [Logout](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/logout)
 function in your program to ensure sessions are ending once the user exits the program.

Killing a session[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/sessions#killing-a-session)

=======================================================================================

A session can be killed by:

calling the [Logout](https://docs.keyauth.cc/api/getting-started/logout)
 function within the program

manually killing via the [sessions page](https://keyauth.cc/app/?page=sessions)

Chats
=====

What are chats?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/chats#what-are-chats)

===============================================================================

Chats aka chatrooms allow users to communicate with each other within a program.

Creating a chatroom[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/chats#creating-a-chatroom)

========================================================================================

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373452/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

When creating a chatroom, you will be able to assign the following:

Channel name: The name of the chatroom where users will send/receive messages

Cooldown Unit/Duration: The amount of time between messages from a user.

Muting a user[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/chats#muting-a-user)

============================================================================

To mute a user, will need to head over to the [messages tab](https://docs.keyauth.cc/**https:/keyauth.cc/app/?page=chats#messages**)
 , selecting a message that was sent by the user and then selecting `mute user`. It will then ask you how long you would like to mute the user for.

Unmuting a user[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/chats#unmuting-a-user)

================================================================================

To unmute a user, will need to head over to the [messages tab](https://docs.keyauth.cc/**https:/keyauth.cc/app/?page=chats#messages**)
 , selecting a message that was sent by the user and then selecting `unmute user`.

Licenses
========

What are licenses?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/licenses#what-are-licenses)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Licenses allow your users to login/register on applications. If you would like your users to access your program using a license only, then you will want to use the `.license` function. This will mark the license as used and allow the user to sign in. You may also use the `.register` function which will allow users to enter a username and password in addition to their license to access your program.

Creating a license[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/licenses#creating-a-license)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/372976/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

When creating a license, you are able to assign the following:

License amount: The amount of licenses you would like to create

License mask: The format of the license being created. You can use asterisks (\*) to generate random characters in the mask

Lowercase letters: Will the license contain lowercase letters?

Uppercase letters: Will the license contain uppercase letters?

Subscription level: The subscription that the user is having assigned to their account. Learn more about subscriptions on the subscriptions page.

License note: Additional information you would like to provide on the user. This will help for identification or internal notes.

Expiry unit: Minutes, hours, weeks, etc.

Expiry duration: Expiry duration \* Expiry Unit = Expiration. **Expiry Unit:** Week \* **Expiry Duration:** 5, this will result in an expiration of 5 weeks.

License Status[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/licenses#license-status)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Unused Licenses

When a license is first created, the status will be Not Used  
![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/372978/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Used Licenses

When a license is used, the status will switch to used. If using license login/register only, then the license will turn into a user and appear on the users page.  
![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/372979/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Banned Licenses

Banning the license from the licenses page will only make it so that the license cannot be used to register. If the license is already used and you would like to ban it, then you must do so via the users page or check the "ban user too" checkbox when banning the license.  
![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/372980/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Extending unusued licenses[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/licenses#extending-unusued-licenses)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If needed, unused licenses can be extended. We only allow unused licenses to be extended because if the license is used, then it will appear on the users page and will have to be extended from that page.

License notes[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/licenses#license-notes)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Adding a note to a license will help identify the license better.

Webhooks
========

💡

### Create Webhook[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/webhooks#create-webhook)

Opens a form to create a webhook.

**Webhook Name** - The name of the webhook you are creating.

**Webhook Endpoint** - The endpoint of the webhook you are creating.

**User Agent** - The user agent that the webhook will be sent as.

**Authenticated** - Indicates whether the endpoint is authenticated with HTTPS or not.

💡

### Delete All Webhooks[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/webhooks#delete-all-webhooks)

Deletes all webhooks.

💡

### Table Contents[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/webhooks#table-contents)

This table provides control and information about your webhooks.

**Select** - Select multiple webhooks.

**ID** - The webhook ID.

**Endpoint** - The webhook endpoint.

**User-agent** - The webhook user-agent.

**Authenticated** - Authentication status (true/false).

**Actions** - This is a dropdown menu of actions you can perform on the webhooks.

**Delete Webhook** - Deletes the webhook.

**Edit Webhook** - Edits the webhook.

Subscriptions
=============

What are subscriptions?[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/subscriptions#what-are-subscriptions)

=======================================================================================================

Subscriptions are used to better organize users as well as set custom expiration dates for users.

Creating a subscription[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/subscriptions#creating-a-subscription)

========================================================================================================

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373379/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)  
When creating a subscription, you will be able to assign the following:

Subscription name: The name of the subscription

Subscription level: The level of the subscription. Levels can play a huge part when it comes to allowing resellers certain permissions.

Subscription Status[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/subscriptions#subscription-status)

================================================================================================

No Users

The subscription isn't assigned to any user.

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373380/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Active

The subscription is assigned to one or more users.

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373381/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Paused

The subscription is paused for all assigned users.

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373384/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Pausing a subscription[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/subscriptions#pausing-a-subscription)

======================================================================================================

When pausing a subscription, every user that has that subscription will not be able to access their account, unless they have another subscription assigned. Example:

Assigning a subscription to a user[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/subscriptions#assigning-a-subscription-to-a-user)

==============================================================================================================================

It is a common misunderstanding that some users may want to add a subscription to a user via the [subscriptions page](https://keyauth.cc/app/?page=subscriptions)
, instead adding a subscription to a user should be done on the [users page](https://keyauth.cc/app/?page=users)
.

![image.png](https://docs.keyauth.cc/raiz5jee8eiph0eeFooV/api/v1/projects/447751/resources/373451/image-preview?onlineShareType=apidoc&locale=en-US)

Clicking the blue `extend expiration` button will allow you to select:

the user(s) the subscription is being assigned to

the subscription being assigned to the user(s)

the expiration of the subscription being assigned to the user(s)

active users only toggle. If this is toggled, then only users with an active subscription will receive the additional subscription/time added to them.

Deleting a subscription[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/subscriptions#deleting-a-subscription)

========================================================================================================

When a subscription is deleted, it is removed from all users who have it assigned to them. All subscriptions except for the `default` subscription can be deleted.

📌

Users cannot log in without a valid subscription.

Files
=====

💡

### Upload File[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/files#upload-file)

Opens a form to enter your direct download link.

💡

### Delete All Files[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/files#delete-all-files)

Deletes all files on your application.

💡

### Table Contents[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/files#table-contents)

This table provides control and information about your files.

**Select** - Select multiple files.

**Filename** - The name of the file.

**ID** - The file ID.

**Size** - The size of the file.

**Upload Date** - The date the file was uploaded.

**Authenticated** - Authentication status (true/false).

**Actions** - This is a dropdown menu of actions you can perform on the files.

**Delete File** - Deletes the file.

**Download File** - Downloads the file.

**Edit File** - Edits the file.

Audit Logs
==========

💡

### Export Audit Logs[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/audit-logs#export-audit-logs)

Downloads a JSON file with your audit logs, named KeyAuthAuditLogs.json.

💡

### Delete All Audit Logs[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/audit-logs#delete-all-audit-logs)

Deletes all audit logs.

💡

### Table Contents[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/audit-logs#table-contents)

This table provides information about the logs.

**User** - The user that triggered the log entry.

**Event** - The event that triggered the log entry.

**Time** - The date and time the log entry was created.

Global Variables
================

💡

### Create Account[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/variables#create-account)

Opens a form to create a global variable.

**Variable Name** - The name of the variable you are creating.

**Variable Data** - The data of the variable you are creating.

💡

### Delete All Variables[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/variables#delete-all-variables)

Deletes all global variables.

💡

### Table Contents[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/variables#table-contents)

This table provides information and control over your global variables.

**Variable Name** - The name of the variable.

**Variable Data** - The data of the variable.

**Authenticated** - Whether or not the variable is authenticated.

**Actions** - This is a dropdown menu of actions you can perform on the variable.

**Delete Global Variable** - Deletes the global variable.

**Edit Global Variable** - Edits the global variable.

Blacklists
==========

💡

### Create Blacklist[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/blacklists#create-blacklist)

Opens a form to add a blacklist entry.

**Blacklist Type** - The type of blacklist you are adding.

**IP Address** - IP Address of a user to blacklist.

**Hardware ID** - Hardware ID of a user to blacklist.

**Region/State** - Region/State to blacklist.

**Country Code** - Country Code to blacklist.

**ASN Number** - ASN Number to blacklist.

**Enter blacklist data** - The data you are blacklisting.

**Reason** - The reason for blacklisting.

💡

### Delete All Blacklists[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/blacklists#delete-all-blacklists)

Deletes all blacklisted data.

💡

### Table Contents[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/blacklists#table-contents)

This table provides control and information about the blacklists.

**Selection** - Select multiple blacklist entries.

**Blacklist Data** - The data that is blacklisted.

**Blacklist Type** - The type of data that is blacklisted.

**Reason** - The reason the data was blacklisted.

**Actions** - This is a dropdown menu of actions you can perform on the blacklisted data.

**Delete Blacklist** - Deletes the blacklist entry.

Logs
====

💡

### About Logs[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/logs#about-logs)

Logs can be sent from anywhere in your program. After login, register, when a user presses a button, etc. There are two types of logs that we currently support:

**Dashboard Logs** - These logs are sent to your dashboard. There is a limit of 100,000 logs.

**Discord Logs** - These logs are sent to a Discord server of your choosing. If you send logs to Discord, they will NOT be stored on the dashboard. To enable Discord logs, head over to the [application settings page](https://keyauth.cc/app/?page=app-settings)
 and on the "Application Functions" tab, you will see a field for "Discord Webhook Link".

💡

### Export Logs[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/logs#export-logs)

Export all of your application logs.

💡

### Delete All Logs[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/logs#delete-all-logs)

Deletes all logs stored on the dashboard.

💡

### Table Contents[#](https://docs.keyauth.cc/dashboard/app/logs#table-contents)

This table provides information and control over your application logs.

**Log Date** - The date the log occurred.

**Log Data** - The data the log is sending through.

**Credential** - The username of the user who is having the log sent through.

**Device Name** - The device of the user who is having the log sent through..
